##### Luxury brands went all-in on AI slop right before Christmas; meanwhile, some brands embraced human craft and emotions. It’s the last creative advertising roundup of 2025. Make it count!

*This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/b89c6c06-e2e9-4b8f-9caf-2eb3142182df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the always-on, affordable brand tracking dashboard that helps marketers and agencies prove the impact of brand building over time. **Now tracking 25 markets (and growing), giving you consistent brand health data everywhere you operate.***

> Brand & Advertising coverage from 07.12 — 19.12.2025, with a new section covering the ongoing Christmas season.

*In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/b89c6c06-e2e9-4b8f-9caf-2eb3142182df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)*

##### **Hey, folks…before you close the laptop for the year, a quick question:**

Do you actually know what your brand health looks like heading into 2026?

December’s when the plans get sharpened, the [budgets get debated](https://substack.com/redirect/36eac3e3-b9a7-4a55-9666-845d267e5095?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), and the brave ideas start jostling for space. But the brands who hit the ground running in January all have one thing in common: they already have their always-on brand tracking sorted.

[Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/b89c6c06-e2e9-4b8f-9caf-2eb3142182df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) gives you fresh brand insights every month — your funnel, your category, your competitors — so you’re not starting next year with guesswork. Just crisp insights you can act on and share with the whole team.

If 2025 was the year of reacting, make 2026 the year of leading. Give your team the gift of always-on brand insight.

To give you a feel for it, Ed from Tracksuit has done us a solid and filmed [a quick dashboard walkthrough](https://substack.com/redirect/125cbd59-641c-4c2f-8d02-bab6c6849217?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) featuring the (arguably) coolest bank ever, **Monzo.**

> Or, you can just skip straight to [booking in your own dashboard demo](https://substack.com/redirect/7aa5176f-dbfe-4248-a1c3-a69eacdcc4a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

> 🎄 The Case for Christmas.

> £20bn set to be spent on [Christmas groceries](https://substack.com/redirect/84e817ee-577f-429c-bb74-029900f7111a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) yet UK consumers prioritise affordability. 40% of UK households plan to use saved loyalty points and vouchers and 23% will shop around for promotions to maximise savings over Christmas

> Ocado (+14.0%) still leads the pack in retailer growth over the last twelve weeks, while Lidl (+9.8%) and Marks & Spencer (+9.2%) maintain good momentum

> + Supermarket Christmas ads: [consumer awareness](https://substack.com/redirect/c70ac5fb-2018-4a50-887f-92cb0a6a5a33?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) falling each year.

> 🫩 Humor, music and nostalgia rule Super Bowl ads in [new poll](https://substack.com/redirect/96870f09-d83e-4edb-ae22-2a9473420de8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

> 🎛️ Report Break: New research has identified a significant “[intention gap](https://substack.com/redirect/ac6f8e52-1e3d-4060-a4ff-d971ffc42e9c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” in Gen Z’s sustainable shopping habits, with almost six in 10 (59%) young consumers admitting their generation talks more about sustainability than they practise.

<h1 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;color: rgb(54,55,55);font-family: 'SF Pro Display', -apple-system, system-ui, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji', 'Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 900;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 0;line-height: 36px;font-size: 32px;"><a href="https://substack.com/app-link/post?publication\_id=712314&amp;post\_id=182081171&amp;utm\_source=post-email-title&amp;utm\_campaign=email-post-title&amp;isFreemail=true&amp;r=6q6egl&amp;token=eyJ1c2VyX2lkIjo0MDY3NjU3NDksInBvc3RfaWQiOjE4MjA4MTE3MSwiaWF0IjoxNzY2MTg1ODM1LCJleHAiOjE3Njg3Nzc4MzUsImlzcyI6InB1Yi03MTIzMTQiLCJzdWIiOiJwb3N0LXJlYWN0aW9uIn0.KrGD0w-wQY2T86xzudYfeZT6RIX6XMJK9oinTdGrC7g" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: none;">The week that changed luxury fashion: a final call for the best and worst of marketing.</a></h1>
<h3 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: normal;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 4px 0 0;color: #777777;line-height: 24px;font-size: 18px;margin-top: 12px;">Luxury brands went all-in on AI slop right before Christmas; meanwhile, some brands embraced human craft and emotions. <div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: Spectral,sans-serif;font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><em><span>This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b89c6c06-e2e9-4b8f-9caf-2eb3142182df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the always-on, affordable brand tracking dashboard that helps marketers and agencies prove the impact of brand building over time.</span><strong> Now tracking 25 markets (and growing), giving you consistent brand health data everywhere you operate.</strong></em></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Brand &amp; Advertising coverage from 07.12 — 19.12.2025, with a new section covering the ongoing Christmas season. </p></blockquote>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/bec55781","text":"The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/031abad0-ef07-4bbe-9eb4-a47ef4128c7e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)</span></a></p>

<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><em><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b89c6c06-e2e9-4b8f-9caf-2eb3142182df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a></em></p>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;"><strong>Hey, folks…before you close the laptop for the year, a quick question:</strong></h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Do you actually know what your brand health looks like heading into 2026?</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>December’s when the plans get sharpened, the</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/36eac3e3-b9a7-4a55-9666-845d267e5095?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> budgets get debated</a><span>, and the brave ideas start jostling for space. But the brands who hit the ground running in January all have one thing in common: they already have their always-on brand tracking sorted.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b89c6c06-e2e9-4b8f-9caf-2eb3142182df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span> gives you fresh brand insights every month — your funnel, your category, your competitors — so you’re not starting next year with guesswork. Just crisp insights you can act on and share with the whole team.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">If 2025 was the year of reacting, make 2026 the year of leading. Give your team the gift of always-on brand insight.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>To give you a feel for it, Ed from Tracksuit has done us a solid and filmed </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/125cbd59-641c-4c2f-8d02-bab6c6849217?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a quick dashboard walkthrough</a><span> featuring the (arguably) coolest bank ever, </span><strong>Monzo.</strong></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Or, you can just skip straight to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7aa5176f-dbfe-4248-a1c3-a69eacdcc4a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">booking in your own dashboard demo</a><span>. </span></p></blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Ford’s CEO said the</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aa25ebc5-45a5-4b89-9a6a-3b0933f6f8ec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> EV market would be halved</a><span> without subsidies. Now he’s writing down $19.5 billion.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Can </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4c811e6a-d360-4c8b-b4e0-1f644ef8ad85?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Jollibee</a><span> Beat American Fast Food at Its Own Game?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The projects </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bc658b15-7b51-4777-ba2b-48aa223989ad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">publishers are most proud</a><span> of in 2025.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Rick Owens </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6eb13e4c-a8b9-4f5f-96f1-e8020de6f995?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">removes</a><span> animal fur from future collections.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>During the first six weeks of the holiday shopping season, returns of online purchases were </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/36c4b3ce-40f8-49aa-9570-bc083a324bd5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">down 2.5%</a><span>, Adobe reported today.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok signs deal to sell US unit in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/800e3fb4-3682-4856-952d-110015b955a4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">joint venture deal</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>👀 $1 billion in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b66a0ca9-7c33-4189-befd-aae2080520de?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">de minimis tariff revenue</a><span> has been collected since loophole closed.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>EU '</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/90335e29-ba6c-40d5-aadd-371f61dcef6d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">veggie burger</a><span>' ban stalls after talks collapse.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b7bceaca-85d2-4015-83db-ba44b916d39a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Orwellian</a><span>’: Ben &amp; Jerry’s founder tells CNBC he’s alarmed by</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bab635ed-a7f2-481a-8520-c5ac6dd9cb80?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> Magnum’s board overhaul</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meet The Bubble Girls. (</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a910f239-344a-49f2-a1da-63e955e2581d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">read</a><span>)</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0944ea5c-d147-45bc-9570-054cb0f68278?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Autistica</a><span> launches a fundraising and awareness campaign that</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/937d3d21-dd9a-4d96-8f52-73949f0ef386?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> calls for calm </a><span>this Christmas.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">🎄 The Case for Christmas.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>£20bn set to be spent on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/84e817ee-577f-429c-bb74-029900f7111a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Christmas groceries</a><span> yet UK consumers prioritise affordability. 40% of UK households plan to use saved loyalty points and vouchers and 23% will shop around for promotions to maximise savings over Christmas</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Ocado (+14.0%) still leads the pack in retailer growth over the last twelve weeks, while Lidl (+9.8%) and Marks &amp; Spencer (+9.2%) maintain good momentum</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>+ Supermarket Christmas ads: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c70ac5fb-2018-4a50-887f-92cb0a6a5a33?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">consumer awareness</a><span> falling each year.</span></p>
</blockquote>

Every brand is now engaging with GenAI, and some are trying it for the sake of trying, without a clear intent.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Brands that have posted AI-generated content in one way or another: Prada, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/209df628-24bc-4534-9231-b35c5f161651?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Balenciaga</a><span>, Guess, Loewe, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e530edbd-1262-482b-a9df-b0344dc786cc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Moncler</a><span>, Gucci, Boss, Burberry, and counting. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Valentino criticised over ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/66d4b888-4465-4f5a-b851-7f01dcbbb91a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">disturbing</a><span>’ AI handbag ads.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0a09bd3f-56c5-420b-9f7d-354e28809a0e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Zara turns to AI </a><span>to generate fashion imagery using real-life models.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>

<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🫩 Humor, music and nostalgia rule Super Bowl ads in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/96870f09-d83e-4edb-ae22-2a9473420de8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new poll</a><span>.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">According to Platinum Rye Entertainment’s fourth annual PRE Super Poll, 18 percent of viewers tune in mainly for the ads, while 80 percent say they watch every commercial during the game.</p>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a2b26358-5eb7-48d1-a777-48b23fa9fe29?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Premier Inn</a><span> hires Born Social digital agency for social media blitz.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🎛️ Report Break: New research has identified a significant “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ac6f8e52-1e3d-4060-a4ff-d971ffc42e9c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">intention gap</a><span>” in Gen Z’s sustainable shopping habits, with almost six in 10 (59%) young consumers admitting their generation talks more about sustainability than they practise.</span></p></blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Back on Sunday. Until then… </p>
